- 4439 files · ~10,484,146 words

- 64524 nodes · 182249 edges · 1861 communities (1209 shown, 652 thin omitted)

- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `e1aae99d`

- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
